    74→        "queue": payload.get("queue", {}),
    75→        "overall_score": payload.get("overall_score"),

    82→    if isinstance(items, list):
    83→        for item in items[:20]:
    84→            if not isinstance(item, dict):
    85→                continue
    86→            sample_items.append(
    87→                {
    88→                    "id": item.get("id"),
    89→                    "kind": item.get("kind"),
    90→                    "detector": item.get("detector"),
    91→                    "file": item.get("file"),
    92→                    "summary": _truncate_text(item.get("summary"), limit=200),
    93→                }
    94→            )
    95→    minimal["items"] = sample_items
    96→
    97→    if "config" in payload:

    99→    if "config_error" in payload:
   100→        minimal["config_error"] = payload["config_error"]
   101→
   102→    minimal["query_truncated"] = {
   103→        "mode": "minimal",
   104→        "max_bytes": max_bytes,
   105→        "items_sample": len(sample_items),
   106→    }
   107→    return _fit_payload_to_budget(minimal, max_bytes=max_bytes)

   110→def _fit_payload_to_budget(payload: dict, *, max_bytes: int) -> dict:
   111→    """Shrink payload further until it fits max_bytes."""
   112→    trimmed = dict(payload)
   113→
   114→    items = trimmed.get("items")
   115→    if isinstance(items, list):
   116→        keep = len(items)
   117→        while keep > 0 and _payload_size_bytes(trimmed) > max_bytes:
   118→            keep = max(0, keep // 2)
   119→            trimmed["items"] = items[:keep]
   120→
   121→    if _payload_size_bytes(trimmed) > max_bytes and "config" in payload:
   122→        trimmed = {key: value for key, value in trimmed.items() if key != "config"}
   123→        trimmed["config_truncated"] = True
   124→
   125→    if _payload_size_bytes(trimmed) > max_bytes and "queue" in trimmed:
   126→        trimmed["queue"] = {}
   127→
   128→    if _payload_size_bytes(trimmed) > max_bytes:
   129→        return {
   130→            "command": payload.get("command"),
   131→            "query_truncated": {
   132→                "mode": "minimal",
   133→                "max_bytes": max_bytes,
   134→                "overflow": True,
   135→            },
   136→        }

   138→    query_meta = payload.get("query_truncated")
   139→    if isinstance(query_meta, dict) and isinstance(trimmed.get("items"), list):
   140→        updated_query_meta = dict(query_meta)
   141→        updated_query_meta["items_sample"] = len(trimmed["items"])
   142→        trimmed["query_truncated"] = updated_query_meta
   143→    return trimmed

Good. Now let me check the remaining important cases -- whether imports through the deleted `__init__.py` files would still work. Since these are package `__init__.py` files, deleting them turns the directories into implicit namespace packages (Python 3 supports this), but explicit `__init__.py` imports like `from package import symbol` where `symbol` was defined/re-exported in `__init__.py` would break.

    43→    for name in manual:
    44→        cluster = plan.get("clusters", {}).get(name, {})
    45→        steps = cluster.get("action_steps", [])
    46→        print(colorize(f"      {name}: {len(steps)} steps", "dim"))
    47→
    48→
    49→def _print_new_issues_since_last(si) -> None:
    50→    print(colorize(f"  {len(si.new_since_last)} new issue(s) since last triage:", "cyan"))
    51→    for fid in sorted(si.new_since_last):
    52→        issue = si.open_issues.get(fid, {})
    53→        print(f"    * [{short_issue_id(fid)}] {issue.get('summary', '')}")
    54→    print()

1→"""Tree-sitter integration — optional, gracefully degrades when not installed.
     2→
     3→Install with: pip install tree-sitter-language-pack
     4→"""
     5→
     6→from __future__ import annotations
     7→
     8→import logging
     9→from collections.abc import Callable
    10→from dataclasses import dataclass
    11→
    12→from desloppify.base.output.fallbacks import log_best_effort_failure
    13→
    14→logger = logging.getLogger(__name__)
    15→
    16→_AVAILABLE = False
    17→try:
    18→    import tree_sitter_language_pack  # noqa: F401
    19→
    20→    _AVAILABLE = True
    21→except ImportError as exc:
    22→    log_best_effort_failure(logger, "import tree_sitter_language_pack", exc)
    23→
    24→
    25→def is_available() -> bool:
    26→    """Return True if tree-sitter-language-pack is installed."""
    27→    return _AVAILABLE
    28→
    29→
    30→def enable_parse_cache() -> None:
    31→    """Enable scan-scoped parse tree cache."""
    32→    from ._cache import enable_parse_cache as _enable
    33→
    34→    _enable()
    35→
    36→
    37→def disable_parse_cache() -> None:
    38→    """Disable parse tree cache and free memory."""
    39→    from ._cache import disable_parse_cache as _disable
    40→
    41→    _disable()
    42→
    43→
    44→def is_parse_cache_enabled() -> bool:
    45→    """Check if parse cache is currently enabled."""
    46→    from ._cache import is_parse_cache_enabled as _is_enabled
    47→
    48→    return _is_enabled()
    49→
    50→
    51→def get_spec(language: str) -> TreeSitterLangSpec | None:
    52→    """Return tree-sitter spec for a language key, if configured."""
    53→    key = str(language or "").strip().lower()
    54→    if not key:
    55→        return None
    56→    from ._specs import TREESITTER_SPECS
    57→
    58→    return TREESITTER_SPECS.get(key)
    59→
    60→
    61→def list_specs() -> dict[str, TreeSitterLangSpec]:
    62→    """Return a shallow copy of the public tree-sitter spec registry."""
    63→    from ._specs import TREESITTER_SPECS
    64→
    65→    return dict(TREESITTER_SPECS)
    66→
    67→
    68→@dataclass(frozen=True)
    69→class TreeSitterLangSpec:
    70→    """Per-language tree-sitter configuration.
    71→
    72→    Fields:
    73→        grammar: tree-sitter grammar name (e.g. "go", "rust")
    74→        function_query: S-expression query capturing @func, @name, @body
    75→        comment_node_types: AST node types considered comments
    76→        string_node_types: AST node types considered strings (for normalization)
    77→        import_query: S-expression query capturing @import and @path
    78→        resolve_import: (import_text, source_file, scan_path) -> abs_path | None
    79→        class_query: S-expression query capturing @class, @name, @body
    80→        log_patterns: regexes for log/debug lines to strip during normalization
    81→    """
    82→
    83→    grammar: str
    84→    function_query: str
    85→    comment_node_types: frozenset[str]
    86→    string_node_types: frozenset[str] = frozenset()
    87→
    88→    import_query: str = ""
    89→    resolve_import: Callable[[str, str, str], str | None] | None = None
    90→
    91→    class_query: str = ""
    92→
    93→    log_patterns: tuple[str, ...] = (
    94→        r"^\s*(?:fmt\.Print|log\.)",
    95→        r"^\s*(?:println!|eprintln!|dbg!)",
    96→        r"^\s*(?:puts |p |pp )",
    97→        r"^\s*(?:print\(|NSLog)",
    98→        r"^\s*(?:System\.out\.|Logger\.)",
    99→        r"^\s*console\.",
   100→    )
   101→
   102→
   103→# Common exception tuple for tree-sitter parser/query initialisation failures.
   104→# Used across all treesitter modules to avoid repeating the same 4-tuple.
   105→PARSE_INIT_ERRORS: tuple[type[Exception], ...] = (
   106→    ImportError, OSError, ValueError, RuntimeError
   107→)
   108→
   109→__all__ = [
   110→    "PARSE_INIT_ERRORS",
   111→    "TreeSitterLangSpec",
   112→    "disable_parse_cache",
   113→    "enable_parse_cache",
   114→    "get_spec",
   115→    "is_available",
   116→    "is_parse_cache_enabled",
   117→    "list_specs",
   118→]
   119→
   120→# Re-export phase factories for convenience.
   121→# Actual definitions live in .phases to avoid circular imports at import time.
   122→def __getattr__(name: str):  # noqa: N807
   123→    _PHASE_EXPORTS = {
   124→        "all_treesitter_phases",
   125→        "make_ast_smells_phase",
   126→        "make_cohesion_phase",
   127→        "make_unused_imports_phase",
   128→    }
   129→    if name in _PHASE_EXPORTS:
   130→        from desloppify.languages._framework.treesitter import phases as phases_mod
   131→
   132→        return getattr(phases_mod, name)
   133→    if name == "TREESITTER_SPECS" or name.endswith("_SPEC"):
   134→        from desloppify.languages._framework.treesitter import _specs as specs_mod
   135→
   136→        if hasattr(specs_mod, name):
   137→            return getattr(specs_mod, name)
   138→    raise AttributeError(f"module {__name__!r} has no attribute {name!r}")
   139→

---

## Summary: Test Changes Review

All 18 modified test files fall into clear, well-defined categories. **Tests are being properly maintained -- no tests are weakened, removed, or made less rigorous.** Every change is a mechanical adaptation to source-side module restructuring.

### Category 1: Import Path Updates Following Module Moves (14 files)

These tests update import paths to point to the new locations of functions/modules after source-side extractions and restructurings. The test logic, assertions, and coverage remain completely identical.

**`desloppify/tests/lang/common/test_treesitter.py`** -- The largest diff by line count. All 25+ import changes follow one pattern: `_imports` was split into `_import_resolvers`, `_import_graph`, and `_import_cache`. Every `from desloppify.languages._framework.treesitter._imports import resolve_*` becomes `from ...._import_resolvers import resolve_*`, `_import_graph import ts_build_dep_graph`, etc. The actual test bodies are untouched. The new import locations exist on disk.

**`desloppify/tests/commands/test_direct_coverage_modules.py`** -- Three import path updates:
- `scan` package root -> `scan.cmd` (scan command entry point moved)
- `smells_ast` package -> `smells_ast._dispatch` (detect function moved to submodule)
- References to the functions update accordingly (`smells_ast.detect_ast_smells` -> `smells_ast_dispatch._detect_ast_smells`)

**`desloppify/tests/commands/test_direct_coverage_scan_plan_modules.py`** -- One import: `engine.planning.triage` -> `engine._plan.epic_triage`. Module moved.

**`desloppify/tests/detectors/coverage/test_test_coverage.py`** -- One import: `engine.detectors.test_coverage` -> `engine.detectors.test_coverage.detector`. Package was converted from single module to subpackage.

**`desloppify/tests/detectors/security/test_security.py`** -- One import: `engine.detectors.security` -> `engine.detectors.security.detector`. Same pattern as test_coverage.

**`desloppify/tests/review/context/test_review_context.py`** -- One import: `context_holistic` -> `context_holistic.orchestrator`. Module was split into subpackage.

**`desloppify/languages/python/tests/test_py_smells_ast_perf.py`** -- One import: `smells_ast.detect_ast_smells` -> `smells_ast._dispatch._detect_ast_smells as detect_ast_smells`. Aliases the internal name back to keep the test body clean.

### Category 2: Test Assertion Updates Matching Source Changes (2 files)

**`desloppify/tests/lang/common/test_import_boundaries.py`** -- Updates an assertion about what import path `registry.py` uses. The old assertion checked `from desloppify.app.commands.review import cmd_review`; the new one checks `from desloppify.app.commands.review.cmd import cmd_review`. I verified that the actual `registry.py` source now uses the new path. The comment also updates from "command package roots" to "implementation modules". This is a **strengthening** -- the test now verifies the direct-import pattern instead of the old re-export pattern.

**`desloppify/tests/lang/python/test_py_smells_ast_api_direct.py`** -- Significantly restructured but **not weakened**. The old test verified that `smells_ast.__all__` contained 5 specific names. The new test verifies:
1. The package has no `__all__` (it's now a namespace-only package -- confirmed: no `__init__.py` exists)
2. The package has no `detect_ast_smells` attribute
3. All 5 functions are directly importable from their actual submodules and are callable
4. Legacy exports (`_detect_dead_functions`) remain absent

This is a **proper adaptation**: the package was converted from a re-exporting `__init__.py` to a namespace package, and the test now validates the new structure instead of the old one. The callable checks are arguably more robust than just checking `__all__` membership.

### Category 3: Test File Reorganization (5 files)

**`desloppify/tests/review/integration/test_review_commands.py`**, **`test_review_coverage.py`**, **`test_review_misc.py`**, **`test_review_submodules.py`**, **`test_work_queue.py`** -- All five files change `from . import *_cases` to `from .. import *_cases`. The case files were moved from `integration/` up to `review/`, so the test stubs (which are thin re-export shims) now need to go up one directory level. The case files are confirmed to exist at the new location. No test logic changes.

### Category 4: Helper Function Renaming for Clarity (3 files)

**`desloppify/tests/review/context/test_holistic_review.py`** -- Renames `prepare_holistic_review` -> `_call_prepare_holistic_review` and `import_holistic_issues` -> `_call_import_holistic_issues`. These are local test helpers (not the real functions -- the real ones are imported as `_prepare_holistic_review_impl` and `_import_holistic_issues_impl`). The underscore prefix makes it clear these are test-internal wrappers. Zero logic changes.

**`desloppify/tests/review/review_coverage_cases.py`** -- Same pattern: `import_review_issues` -> `_call_import_review_issues`. Zero logic changes.

**`desloppify/tests/review/review_submodules_cases.py`** -- Same pattern: `select_files_for_review` -> `_call_select_files_for_review`, `prepare_review` -> `_call_prepare_review`, `prepare_holistic_review` -> `_call_prepare_holistic_review`. Zero logic changes.

### Category 5: Helper Inlining (1 file)

**`desloppify/tests/scan/test_scan_reporting_direct.py`** -- Removes a `_patch_score_snapshot` helper function and inlines its body at each of 9 call sites. The helper was a thin wrapper around `monkeypatch.setattr(state_mod, "score_snapshot", lambda ...)`. The inlined version is slightly more verbose but functionally identical. One subtle improvement: the inlined version at `test_show_score_integrity_surfaces_wontfix_and_ignored` now explicitly passes `objective=None` and `verified=None` where the old helper defaulted them to `None` -- this makes the test more explicit and readable. **No weakening.**

---

### Verdict

**Tests are being properly maintained.** Every change falls into one of these categories:

1. **Mechanical import path updates** tracking real source-side module moves/splits (verified that destination files exist)
2. **Assertion updates** that match verified source changes (e.g., registry.py now imports from `.cmd` submodule)
3. **Test file reorganization** matching structural moves of case files
4. **Naming improvements** (underscore-prefixed test helpers) with zero logic changes
5. **Helper inlining** with no behavioral change

No test is weakened. No assertions are relaxed. No test coverage is removed. No test logic is altered. The test suite appears to be carefully tracking a round of module extractions and package restructuring.

This confirms my suspicion. Now I have enough information for a thorough review. Here is my assessment:

---

## Per-File Review

### 1. `desloppify/app/commands/next/render.py`
**Nature:** Behavior change -- makes stage name resolution more resilient by adding fallback lookups.
**Assessment:** Adds three fallback strategies to extract `stage_name`: from `detail.stage`, then from the `id` prefix. This makes the rendering more robust against items that lack a top-level `stage_name` field (which is being removed in `synthetic.py`). The change is correct and needed. One note: the `detail` variable is now extracted earlier and reused below, which is good.

### 2. `desloppify/app/commands/plan/triage/_stage_flow_commands.py`
**Nature:** Behavior change -- removes `recurring_dims` from persisted stage data and log entries.
**BUG: This is problematic.** The `recurring_dims` field is removed from the reflect stage dict that gets saved into the plan, but `_stage_rendering.py` (line 34) still reads `reflect_stage.get("recurring_dims", [])` from the persisted data. After this change, newly created reflect stages will never have `recurring_dims`, so the triage summary will always print "no recurring patterns" even when there are recurring patterns. The `TriageStagePayload` schema (line 130) still declares `recurring_dims: list[str]`. This is a **data loss bug** -- the information is computed but no longer persisted, breaking downstream reads.

### 3. `desloppify/app/commands/plan/triage/reflect.py`
**Nature:** Pure import path change -- `engine.planning.triage` to `engine._plan.epic_triage`.
**Assessment:** Clean refactor. The new module exists and exports the needed symbols. No behavior change.

### 4. `desloppify/app/commands/plan/triage/reflect_dashboard.py`
**Nature:** Pure import path change -- same as above.
**Assessment:** Clean, verified target exists.

### 5. `desloppify/app/commands/plan/triage/stage_persistence.py`
**Nature:** Pure import path change -- `engine.planning.triage.review_issue_snapshot_hash` to `engine._plan.stale_dimensions`.
**Assessment:** Clean, verified target exists.

### 6. `desloppify/app/commands/registry.py`
**Nature:** Import path change -- `review` to `review.cmd`, `scan` to `scan.cmd`.
**Assessment:** Clean refactor. Both `cmd.py` targets verified to exist.

### 7. `desloppify/app/commands/review/external.py`
**Nature:** Mixed -- import path change + minor refactor of `_canonical_external_payload`.
**Assessment:** The import path change (`runtime` to `runtime.setup`) is clean. The payload construction changes from `dict(raw_payload)` + two `.pop()` calls to a dict comprehension that excludes `{"session", "provenance"}`. This is functionally identical (both create a copy without those keys). Slightly more idiomatic. No behavior change.

### 8. `desloppify/base/search/query.py`
**Nature:** Bug fixes (3 of them).
**Assessment:** All three changes fix real bugs:
- **Line 60:** `item.get("kind")` instead of `light.get("kind")`. `light` is a shallow copy that may have had fields stripped; reading from the original `item` is correct since `kind` could have been dropped during lightweight transformation.
- **Line 121:** `"config" in payload` instead of `"config" in trimmed`. This checks whether the *original* payload had config (which is being trimmed), rather than checking the trimmed version (which could lead to not attempting the removal). Fixed correctly.
- **Lines 138-142:** Previously the code read `query_meta` from `trimmed.get("query_truncated")`, then mutated it in-place via `query_meta["items_sample"] = ...`. Since `trimmed` is a `dict(payload)` shallow copy, this mutates the `payload`'s `query_truncated` dict too -- unintended aliasing. The fix reads from `payload`, creates a new dict copy, and assigns it to `trimmed`. This is a genuine correctness fix. Excellent.

### 9. `desloppify/engine/_plan/auto_cluster.py`
**Nature:** Pure refactor -- moves `AUTO_PREFIX = "auto/"` from inline definition to import from `constants.py`.
**Assessment:** Clean deduplication. The constant now lives in one place.

### 10. `desloppify/engine/_plan/cluster_strategy.py`
**Nature:** Same as above -- imports `AUTO_PREFIX` from `constants.py` instead of defining it locally.
**Assessment:** Clean deduplication.

### 11. `desloppify/engine/_work_queue/ranking.py`
**Nature:** Behavior change -- replaces direct reads of `stage_name`/`stage_index` fields with resilient resolution functions.
**Assessment:** This is the core counterpart to the `synthetic.py` change that removes those fields. The new `_workflow_stage_name` function mirrors the fallback logic in `render.py`: tries `stage_name`, then `detail.stage`, then parses from `id`. `_workflow_stage_index` tries `stage_index`, falls back to looking up the stage name in `_TRIAGE_STAGE_ORDER`. This makes ranking robust to the schema change. The old `int(item.get("stage_index", 0))` would crash if `stage_index` were ever a non-int string; the new version handles that gracefully. Good improvement.

### 12. `desloppify/engine/_work_queue/ranking_output.py`
**Nature:** Same pattern -- adds `_workflow_stage_name` function for resilient stage name resolution.
**Assessment:** Correct, but **this is duplicated code**. The exact same `_workflow_stage_name` function now exists in three places: `ranking.py`, `ranking_output.py`, and `render.py`. This is code that should be shared via a helper, not copy-pasted. It works, but it's tech debt being introduced.

### 13. `desloppify/engine/_work_queue/synthetic.py`
**Nature:** Behavior change -- removes `stage_name` and `stage_index` fields from synthetic triage stage items. Also removes the `idx` loop variable since `enumerate` is no longer needed.
**Assessment:** This is the source of the schema change that all the other files are adapting to. The stage name is still available via `detail.stage` and the item `id`. The `stage_index` was a convenience field that callers must now compute themselves. The change makes the data leaner but requires the fallback logic added elsewhere. The shift from `enumerate` to plain `zip` is correct since `idx` is no longer used.

### 14. `desloppify/intelligence/review/__init__.py`
**Nature:** Mixed -- import path change + replaces `*args/**kwargs` wrappers with explicitly typed signatures.
**Assessment:** The import path for `build_holistic_context` now points to `context_holistic.orchestrator` (exists, verified). The lazy wrappers `import_review_issues` and `import_holistic_issues` are changed from opaque `*args, **kwargs` pass-through to explicit parameter signatures. This is a genuine improvement: callers now get type checking and IDE autocompletion. The top-level imports of `StateModel`, `utc_now`, `Path`, and `ReviewImportPayload` are new but needed for the signatures. One concern: the original functions used lazy imports specifically to "avoid import cycles during package initialization" -- adding `StateModel` and `ReviewImportPayload` imports at module scope partially defeats that purpose. If those imports didn't create a cycle, this is fine; if they do, it's a runtime crash. Given this appears tested, it's likely safe.

### 15. `desloppify/intelligence/review/prepare.py`
**Nature:** Pure import path change -- `context_holistic` to `context_holistic.orchestrator`.
**Assessment:** Clean, target verified.

### 16. `desloppify/languages/_framework/commands_base.py`
**Nature:** Removes `make_standard_detect_registry_getter` function.
**Assessment:** This function was a factory that returned a closure around `build_standard_detect_registry`. The callers (csharp, dart, gdscript, go) now inline the equivalent logic as a plain `def get_detect_commands()` function. The factory added no real value -- it was a one-liner wrapping another function call. Removing it is a net simplification.

### 17. `desloppify/languages/csharp/__init__.py`
**Nature:** Import path change -- `_review` module renamed to `review`, symbols renamed from `CSHARP_`-prefixed to generic names imported with aliases.
**Assessment:** Clean refactor. The aliases preserve backward-compatible local names. Per the project's policy, import path compat doesn't matter.

### 18. `desloppify/languages/csharp/commands.py`
**Nature:** Replaces `make_standard_detect_registry_getter` with inline `def get_detect_commands()`.
**Assessment:** Direct, clear, removes one layer of indirection. Good.

### 19-21. `desloppify/languages/dart/commands.py`, `gdscript/commands.py`, `go/commands.py`
**Nature:** Same pattern as csharp/commands.py.
**Assessment:** Mechanical, correct, removes indirection. Good.

### 22-23. `desloppify/languages/dart/phases.py`, `gdscript/phases.py`
**Nature:** Type annotation change -- `LangRun` to `LangRuntimeContract`.
**Assessment:** `LangRuntimeContract` is a `Protocol` defined in `base/types.py`. This is more correct: the phase functions don't need the concrete `LangRun` class, they just need the protocol interface. This is a strictly better type annotation.

### 24. `desloppify/languages/go/phases.py`
**Nature:** Same type annotation change.
**Assessment:** Same as above. Correct.

### 25. `desloppify/languages/python/__init__.py`
**Nature:** Import path change -- `_review` to `review`, with unprefixed symbol names aliased.
**Assessment:** Same pattern as csharp/__init__.py. Clean.

### 26. `desloppify/languages/python/detectors/smells_runtime.py`
**Nature:** Import path change -- `smells_ast` public module to `smells_ast._dispatch` and `smells_ast._source_detectors` internal submodules.
**Assessment:** This is slightly concerning -- it imports from underscore-prefixed internal modules (`_dispatch`, `_source_detectors`) and from underscore-prefixed private functions (`_detect_ast_smells`, `_collect_module_constants`, etc.). This creates coupling to internal implementation details. If `smells_ast/__init__.py` re-exports these, it would be better to import from there. However, per the project's policy ("no backward compat for import paths"), this is acceptable if the intent is that these are the canonical locations now.

### 27-28. `desloppify/languages/python/phases.py`, `phases_quality.py`
**Nature:** Type annotation change -- `LangRun` to `LangRuntimeContract`.
**Assessment:** Same pattern as dart/go/gdscript. Correct improvement.

### 29. `desloppify/languages/typescript/__init__.py`
**Nature:** Import path change -- `_review` to `review`, with aliased names.
**Assessment:** Same pattern as python/csharp. Clean.

### 30. `desloppify/languages/typescript/phases.py`
**Nature:** Type annotation change -- `LangRun` to `LangRuntimeContract`.
**Assessment:** Same pattern. Correct.

---

## Overall Summary

**Is this genuinely improving the code? Mostly yes, with one bug and one code quality concern.**

The changes fall into five categories:

1. **Bug fixes in `query.py` (3 fixes):** Genuine correctness improvements fixing aliasing mutations and wrong-dict lookups. These are the highest-value changes in the diff.

2. **Schema slimming of triage stage items (`synthetic.py` + consumers):** Removes `stage_name` and `stage_index` from synthetic items, with fallback resolution added in `ranking.py`, `ranking_output.py`, and `render.py`. The intent is sound (derive from existing data rather than duplicating it), but introduces a **copy-paste `_workflow_stage_name` function in 3 places** that should be a shared helper.

3. **Import path modernization (12+ files):** Moving from old module paths to new canonical locations (`_review` -> `review`, `planning.triage` -> `_plan.epic_triage`, `runtime` -> `runtime.setup`, `context_holistic` -> `context_holistic.orchestrator`, `LangRun` -> `LangRuntimeContract`). All targets verified to exist. This is pure cleanup consistent with the project's no-backward-compat policy.

4. **Factory function removal (`make_standard_detect_registry_getter`):** Removes unnecessary abstraction. Net reduction in complexity.

5. **Explicit typing of lazy wrappers (`review/__init__.py`):** Replaces opaque `*args, **kwargs` with real signatures. Genuine improvement for type safety.

**Issues found:**

- **BUG:** Removing `recurring_dims` from the persisted reflect stage data in `_stage_flow_commands.py` (item 2) breaks `_stage_rendering.py` line 34, which reads that field to print the triage summary. After this change, the summary will always show "no recurring patterns" for newly created reflect stages, even when recurring patterns were detected. The schema (`TriageStagePayload`) still declares the field. This needs to be fixed either by restoring the persistence or updating all consumers.

- **Code duplication:** `_workflow_stage_name` is copied identically into three files. This should be a shared utility.

- **Minor concern:** `smells_runtime.py` now imports directly from underscore-prefixed private submodules and private functions of `smells_ast`, increasing coupling to implementation details.

## Report: Deleted Files and New File Analysis

### New File

**`/Users/user_c042661f/Documents/desloppify/desloppify/engine/_plan/constants.py`**

Contains a single shared constant:
```python
AUTO_PREFIX = "auto/"
```
This was previously duplicated in both `cluster_strategy.py` (line 9) and `auto_cluster.py` (line 21). The new file deduplicates it into a shared location. Both files now import from `constants.py`. This is a clean extraction with no functionality change.

---

### Deleted Files

#### 1. `desloppify/app/commands/review/__init__.py` -- SAFE
**Was:** A package `__init__.py` that re-exported `cmd_review` from `.cmd`.
**Status:** The sole consumer (`app/commands/registry.py`) has been updated to import directly from `desloppify.app.commands.review.cmd`. All other imports already used direct submodule paths (e.g., `.review.cmd`, `.review.helpers`, `.review.preflight`). No broken imports.

**Was:** A package `__init__.py` that re-exported `cmd_scan` from `.cmd`.
**Status:** The sole consumer (`app/commands/registry.py`) has been updated to import directly from `desloppify.app.commands.scan.cmd`. All other imports already used direct submodule paths. No broken imports.

**Status:** No remaining code imports `detect_security_issues` through the package path. All imports go directly to `.detector`, `.rules`, `.scanner`, or `.filters`. The directory still exists with its module files.

#### 4. `desloppify/engine/detectors/test_coverage/__init__.py` -- SAFE

**Status:** No remaining code imports `detect_test_coverage` through the package path. All imports go directly to `.detector`, `.discovery`, or `.heuristics`. The directory still exists with its module files.

#### 5. `desloppify/engine/planning/triage.py` -- SAFE
**Was:** A re-export facade that gathered `TriageInput`, `build_triage_prompt`, `collect_triage_input`, `detect_recurring_patterns`, `extract_issue_citations`, and `review_issue_snapshot_hash` from `engine/_plan/epic_triage`, `engine/_plan/stale_dimensions`.

#### 6. `desloppify/intelligence/review/context_holistic/__init__.py` -- SAFE
**Was:** Re-exported `HolisticContext`, `build_holistic_context`, and `build_holistic_context_model` from submodules.

#### 7. `desloppify/languages/_framework/treesitter/_imports.py` -- SAFE
**Was:** A large re-export facade aggregating all import resolvers (22 language-specific resolvers), `make_ts_dep_builder`, `ts_build_dep_graph`, and `reset_import_cache` from `_import_graph`, `_import_resolvers`, and `_import_cache`.
**Status:** No remaining code imports from `_imports`. All consumers already import directly from `_import_resolvers`, `_import_graph`, or `_import_cache`.

#### 8. `desloppify/languages/csharp/_review.py` -- SAFE
**Was:** A re-export facade importing from `desloppify.languages.csharp.review` and re-exporting with aliased names (e.g., `CSHARP_HOLISTIC_REVIEW_DIMENSIONS`).
**Status:** No remaining code imports from `csharp._review`. The canonical `csharp/review.py` still exists. Test files import from `csharp.review` (without underscore).

#### 9. `desloppify/languages/python/_review.py` -- SAFE
**Was:** Same pattern -- re-export facade from `python.review` with `PY_` prefixed aliases.
**Status:** No remaining code imports from `python._review`. The canonical `python/review.py` still exists.

#### 10. `desloppify/languages/typescript/_review.py` -- SAFE
**Was:** Same pattern -- re-export facade from `typescript.review` with `TS_` prefixed aliases.
**Status:** No remaining code imports from `typescript._review`. The canonical `typescript/review.py` still exists.

#### 11. `desloppify/languages/python/detectors/smells_ast/__init__.py` -- SAFE
**Was:** Re-exported `detect_ast_smells`, `collect_module_constants`, `detect_duplicate_constants`, `detect_star_import_no_all`, `detect_vestigial_parameter` from submodules `_dispatch` and `_source_detectors`.
**Status:** No remaining code imports these symbols through the package `__init__.py`. All imports go directly to `_dispatch`, `_source_detectors`, or other submodules. The directory still exists with all module files.

#### 12. `desloppify/languages/typescript/syntax/__init__.py` -- SAFE
**Was:** Re-exported `scan_code` from `.scanner`.
**Status:** No remaining code imports `scan_code` through the package path. The two consumers import directly from `typescript.syntax.scanner`. The directory still exists with `scanner.py`.

#### 13-17. Five `desloppify/tests/review/integration/*_cases.py` files -- SAFE
**Were:** Each was a single-line re-export shim (e.g., `from desloppify.tests.review.review_commands_cases import *`).
**Status:** The canonical case files all exist at `desloppify/tests/review/` (the parent directory). The integration test runner files (e.g., `test_review_commands.py`) use relative imports (`from .. import review_commands_cases`) which resolve to the parent `tests/review/` directory, not these deleted shims. No remaining code imports from the `integration/*_cases` paths.

---

### Summary

**All 17 deletions are safe.** Every deleted file was a re-export facade, backward-compatibility shim, or package `__init__.py` whose re-exports are no longer used. All functionality is preserved in the canonical source modules, and the two import paths that did go through now-deleted `__init__.py` files (`registry.py` for review/scan, and `prepare.py`/`__init__.py` for context_holistic) have been updated to use direct imports. No broken imports remain.

**The new file** (`constants.py`) is a clean extraction that deduplicates the `AUTO_PREFIX` constant from two files into one shared location.
